# Tasks: Vue and TypeScript Frontend Migration

**Feature**: `033-frontend-vue-typescript-migration`
**Authoritative rollback source**: `06696ee1c7155a1bb1135ef46ec91445dd73a2a4`
**Execution rule**: run strictly by dependency and wave; a task completes only after its local verification and evidence. Later gates are additive.
**Path rule**: `Files` is the complete modifiable/create/delete inventory; `Read-only` is separately exact; `none` is an empty inventory.
**Parallelism**: no `[P]` tasks—each apparent branch shares a ledger row, legacy monolith, manifest, entrypoint, Taskfile section, generated tree, test configuration, or evidence record.
**ConnectRPC invariant**: all eight RPCs—`Subscribe`, `SelectCharacter`, `Navigate`, `Guess`, `ActivatePattern`, `SetPresentation`, `PresentationUplink`, and `SoundManifest`—retain service paths, messages, field numbers, protobuf authority, binary encoding, cardinalities, Connect errors, typed results, request limits, authorization, capability boundaries, snapshot/revision semantics, snapshot-first streaming, reconnect, backpressure, cancellation, and unary fallback without change.

## User-story delivery map

| Story | Delivery waves | Independent acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| US1 Players retain the terminal experience | c foundation; f–h behavior/cutover; i evidence | Production Player passes unchanged browser/visual, RPC, race, authority, cleanup, serving, and package gates. |
| US2 Overseer retains privileged controls | c–e; i evidence | Production Overseer passes privileged workflow, persistence, boundary, browser/visual, Wails/native, resource, and package gates. |
| US3 Operators retain deployable applications | a–c, e, h, i | Clean separate builds, embeds, contracts, resources, reproducibility, and five package plans pass without capability expansion. |
| US4 Maintainers get strict component applications | a–c, e, h, i | Independent/aggregate SFC checks and policy/boundary checks pass with one workspace and lockfile. |
| US5 Contributors migrate safely | every wave | Each wave has local checks, one owner per subtree, exact temporary ledger, rollback evidence, and a separate exit gate. |

**⟶ Wait for install, then TypeScript configs and direct compiler checks:**

- [x] **T006** [US4] Create the strict capability-neutral compiler base, empty final application programs, and isolated compiler fixtures.
  - Wave: a · Outcome: `frontend/tsconfig.base.json` enables strict/Bundler/ESNext/isolated/verbatim/no-emit, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, and `exactOptionalPropertyTypes` without `allowJs`/`checkJs`; application programs exist but are not falsely treated as populated. The isolated checker compiles valid strict TypeScript, valid strict Vue SFC, and an application-owned declaration successfully; it rejects invalid props with `TS2322`, invalid emits with `TS2345`, unchecked index access with `TS2532`, exact optional assignment with `TS2375`, explicit any with `FTS1001`, suppression with `FTS1002`, and cross-boundary imports with `FTS1003`; any missing compiler/configuration/unrelated diagnostic fails the checker. It operates without production application roots and is executable. The governed no-match helper scans exactly the three readable compiler programs for `allowJs|checkJs` and distinguishes a forbidden match from missing/unreadable input, invalid regex, and `rg`/tool errors. ## Implementation Strategy

Execute one task at a time in the dependency table. Stop at every wave exit on a failed local or integration check. Rollback is source-control reversion to `06696ee1c7155a1bb1135ef46ec91445dd73a2a4`; no runtime toggle, duplicate bundle, second lockfile, second generated format, or permanent coexistence path is permitted. Do not run implementation until this plan has passed `$speckit-analyze`.
